### Scene — The compartment

One place, one stretch of time. Wardrobe holds for the scene unless a panel shows the change.

#### Page 4 — Ron

##### Panel 19

**Picture.** Platform nine and three-quarters. The Hogwarts Express is scarlet and huge, steam blooming under the iron roof. The scarlet is the train, not a house robe. Students in mixed travel clothes say goodbye. No house colors. Cats slip under trolleys. Owls hoot.

**Faces.** Harry looks up at the train, relief and awe. He looks for the red-haired family and finds too many redheads to be sure.

**Caption.** A scarlet steam train. Owls, cats, and steam.

**Dialogue.**

- Conductor: Hogwarts Express! All aboard for Hogwarts!

**Art.** art/c5-p2-p1.jpg

##### Panel 20

**Picture.** An empty compartment. The train lurches and Hedwig's cage slides. Harry catches it. He is still in Dudley's faded shirt and rolled jeans, taped glasses, no school robe. Children in travel clothes and trunks pass in the corridor. The platform slides away outside.

**Faces.** He looks at the empty seat opposite, lonely and proud he managed the trolley. A small smile for Hedwig. She looks back, unruffled.

**Caption.** An empty compartment. The train lurches.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** art/c5-p2-p2.jpg

##### Panel 21

**Picture.** Ron stands in the doorway with a trunk that has a broken corner and a cage holding a fat gray rat. Dirt on his nose. He wears a worn maroon hand-knit with a gold R, and a hand-me-down jacket and trousers. No robe and no tie.

**Faces.** Ron looks at the empty seat, not quite at Harry, shy. Harry looks at Ron, relieved, and nods.

**Caption.** Ron slides the door, shy.

**Dialogue.**

- Ron: Mind if I sit? Everywhere else is full of owls.
- Harry: It's free.

**Art.** art/c5-p2-p3.jpg

##### Panel 22

**Picture.** On the compartment table, Ron's sandwich is a sad curl of corned beef. Harry holds out a box of bright wizard sweets. The trolley witch is just leaving. Countryside smears past the window.

**Faces.** Ron leans in, delighted and a little ashamed of being delighted. Harry is glad to have something to give. This is the first easy smile between them.

**Caption.** Harry offers sweets. Ron lights up.

**Dialogue.**

- Ron: Mum's sandwiches. Always dry.
- Harry: Have a Chocolate Frog. And a Bertie Bott's bean, if you're feeling brave.

**Art.** art/c5-p2-p4.jpg

##### Panel 23

**Picture.** The compartment, moving now, countryside starting to blur. Travel clothes, not robes. A Bertie Bott's bean sits in Ron's fingers. The box is open between them. Scabbers is a fat sleeping rat on a wrapper.

**Faces.** Ron is trying to be brave. Harry is curious and kind.

**Caption.** Ron looks at one bean as if it were a dare.

**Dialogue.**

- Ron: How bad can one bean be?

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 24

**Picture.** Ron puts the bean in his mouth. For a second he looks pleased with himself. Harry watches, the chocolate frog box still shut in his hands.

**Faces.** Ron is cocky for one second. Harry waits.

**Caption.** He eats it.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

#### Page 5 — Beans and a frog

##### Panel 25

**Picture.** Ron's face collapses. He spits into a paper napkin, eyes watering, freckles bright. The bean was foul. A bogey. He is not going to try a second one.

**Faces.** Disgust, then betrayal. Harry looks horrified and a little sorry he offered it.

**Caption.** The flavor is not food.

**Dialogue.**

- Ron: Bogey. That is a bogey. Why is that a sweet?

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 26

**Picture.** Harry lifts the lid of a chocolate frog box. The chocolate inside is shaped like a frog, too perfect, on a card he has not taken. Ron is still green.

**Faces.** Harry thinks it is only chocolate. Ron knows better and is too late.

**Caption.** Harry opens the chocolate frog.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 27

**Picture.** The chocolate frog springs out of the box, a brown blur, legs kicking, box tumbling. Harry's hands are empty. The frog is in the air between them, not a picture of a frog, a frog that jumps.

**Faces.** Harry is startled. Ron's disgust turns into a shout he has not finished.

**Caption.** The frog leaps.

**Dialogue.**

- Harry: It's alive.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 28

**Picture.** The frog has landed on the rack beside the cage, quite pleased. Scabbers snores on the wrapper. Ron, still tasting bogey, stares up. Harry is half out of his seat.

**Faces.** Ron is wretched and impressed. Harry is delighted. The rat does not care. The owl looks unimpressed.

**Caption.** It sits on the luggage rack. Scabbers sleeps through it.

**Dialogue.**

- Ron: Catch it before it finds the sandwiches.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 29

**Picture.** Ron talks with his hands, a sweet half in his mouth. Harry has gone still around an unopened box. The scar shows where his hair parts. Scabbers sits on a wrapper. Green fields rush by.

**Faces.** Ron looks at Harry with the shine of someone meeting a story. Harry looks back, uncomfortable, honestly confused. He does not enjoy the fame.

**Caption.** None.

**Dialogue.**

- Ron: Everyone in my family's been on about you. Harry Potter.
- Harry: I don't know why they would.

**Art.** art/c5-p2-p5.jpg

##### Panel 30

**Picture.** The train enters a tunnel. The window goes dark and their reflections appear. Ron's mouth is open, one hand raised, the explanation stuck. The chocolate box sits unopened between them.

**Faces.** Ron looks kind, and suddenly aware that this is too big for a train game. Harry waits, patient, a little afraid of the answer.

**Caption.** Ron starts to explain, then stops.

**Dialogue.**

- Ron: You-Know-Who killed your parents. And when he tried it on you, he... sorry. That's too big for a train.

**Art.** art/c5-p2-p6.jpg

#### Page 6 — The train corridor

##### Panel 31

**Picture.** A slice of the train's side, the corridor door sliding, other students passing in travel clothes. No house colors. Steam and fields.

**Faces.** The corridor is busy. Our compartment is one door among many.

**Caption.** The scarlet train, from a carriage door.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 32

**Picture.** The narrow corridor, rocking. Cages, a cat basket, a boy patting his pockets in a panic farther down. Compartment doors shut and half shut.

**Faces.** People are settling. One sandy-haired boy is already searching the floor.

**Caption.** The corridor, whole, trunks and owls.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 33

**Picture.** Neville peers under a seat in the corridor, toadless, ears red. A girl with bushy hair and a stack of books comes from the next carriage, already looking too.

**Faces.** Neville is desperate. Hermione is determined. They have not reached Harry's door.

**Caption.** Neville was already on his knees.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 34

**Picture.** Harry and Ron inside, seen through the glass of the sliding door, mid-sweet, the frog maybe still on the rack. Dirt on Ron's nose. They do not see the corridor yet.

**Faces.** They look comfortable. The interruption has not arrived.

**Caption.** Their door, from the corridor side.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 35

**Picture.** Hermione at their door, books clutched, mouth about to ask a question, hand on the handle. Neville is a worried shape behind her, still toadless.

**Faces.** She looks mission-first, not starstruck yet. Neville looks hopeful that anyone might help.

**Caption.** She stops at the door. She has not opened it.

##### Panel 36

**Picture.** The door begins to slide. Harry looks up. Ron has a napkin in his fist from the bean. No one has spoken the toad line. The next panel is her question.

**Faces.** Harry is polite and surprised. Ron is wary. Hermione is about to fill the doorway.

**Caption.** The handle moves.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

#### Page 7 — Hermione and Draco

##### Panel 37

**Picture.** The door slides. Hermione stands there with bushy hair, a thick book, and the face of someone on a mission. Neville panics farther down the corridor, empty-handed.

**Faces.** Hermione looks past them, searching the seats, businesslike. Ron is already overwhelmed. Harry is polite. Neville is near tears.

**Caption.** Hermione opens the door, looking for a toad.

**Dialogue.**

- Hermione: Has anyone seen a toad? A boy called Neville has lost one.

**Art.** art/c5-p3-p1.jpg

##### Panel 38

**Picture.** Hermione's eyes drop to Harry's forehead. Her mission pauses. She leans in, a book under her arm. Ron is a grimace in the background.

**Faces.** Hermione looks at the scar, bright and impressed, no malice. Harry leans back, weary of it already. Ron looks wary.

**Caption.** She sees the scar.

**Dialogue.**

- Hermione: You're Harry Potter. I've read about you. In Rise and Fall of the Dark Arts, and in A History of Magic.

**Art.** art/c5-p3-p2.jpg

##### Panel 39

**Picture.** Hermione walks off down the corridor, one finger raised with the correction, Neville trotting after her. The compartment door stays open.

**Faces.** Hermione looks ahead, satisfied she was right. Ron has both hands over his face. Harry covers his mouth, amused and a little impressed.

**Caption.** She leaves. Ron groans.

**Dialogue.**

- Hermione: It's Wingardium Leviosa, not Leviosar. And you've dirt on your nose, by the way.
- Ron: She has got to be joking.

**Art.** art/c5-p3-p3.jpg

##### Panel 40

**Picture.** The doorway goes dark. Draco stands in front, sleek, hands behind his back, in an expensive plain black coat. No green lining, no snake, no house tie. Crabbe and Goyle fill the frame behind him in bulky dark coats, so there is no leaving.

**Faces.** Draco looks at Harry, assessing, then at Ron's sweater. The big boys look dull. Harry recognizes him from the alley. Ron looks at the blocked door.

**Caption.** Draco enters. Crabbe and Goyle block the door.

**Dialogue.**

- Draco: Potter. Is this compartment taken by Weasleys, or may a decent wizard sit?

**Art.** art/c5-p3-p4.jpg

##### Panel 41

**Picture.** Tight in the compartment. Draco's finger flicks Ron's maroon sleeve. Scabbers is on the floor. Hedwig watches from the rack. Crabbe and Goyle are a wall.

**Faces.** Draco looks at Harry while insulting Ron, recruiting. Ron's ears go red, humiliated and angry. Harry's friendliness is gone.

**Caption.** None.

**Dialogue.**

- Draco: Red hair, a second-hand jumper, and a rat called Scabbers, I shouldn't wonder. You'll regret the company you keep.

**Art.** art/c5-p3-p5.jpg

##### Panel 42

**Picture.** Harry is on his feet, shorter than Draco and square to him. Ron is half-risen. The big boys have shifted, unsure.

**Faces.** Harry looks Draco in the eye, calm, final. Draco's smile is dead, a flicker of insulted surprise. Ron looks at Harry, loyal already.

**Caption.** None.

**Dialogue.**

- Harry: I think I can spot the wrong sort for myself.
- Draco: Have it your way, Potter.

**Art.** art/c5-p3-p6.jpg

#### Page 8 — The journey's end

##### Panel 43

**Picture.** Dusk in the compartment. Lamps on. They sit with their shoes up, empty wrappers on the table, hours passed. Scabbers sleeps on Ron's knee. Hedwig dozes.

**Faces.** They look at each other, easier now. Harry looks grateful. Ron looks indignant on Harry's behalf, which matters more than fame.

**Picture.** Through the window, a forest platform at night. No station building, only lanterns in the trees and a crowd. The scarlet train is huge beside the wood.

**Picture.** On the platform, Hagrid holds a lantern like a small moon and calls the first years. Steam. The scarlet train. Older students head somewhere else. Harry and Ron step down.

**Picture.** Low angle on the night platform. Hagrid's grin fills the top of the panel, lantern beside his cheek. Harry is small at the bottom, in the plain black robe from the compartment, no tie, no crest, and no scarf, Hedwig's cage at his feet. Steam wraps them. The train is a red wall.

**Faces.** Hagrid looks proud, a little emotional, grinning. His hand hovers and does not quite ruffle Harry's hair. Harry looks up, safe, smiling true.

## Chapter 6 — The Sorting Hat

### Scene — The path and the lake

One place, one stretch of time. Wardrobe holds for the scene unless a panel shows the change.

#### Page 1 — Down to the lake

##### Panel 1

**Picture.** From the edge of the grounds, night, the castle is a cluster of lit windows across a black lake. First years are a line of lanterns still up on the path, not at the water yet. Hagrid's lamp is the brightest.

**Faces.** The castle does the feeling. The children are small and cold.

**Caption.** Hogwarts across the water, first sight, night.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 2

**Picture.** A muddy path down through trees, roots, lanterns in a chain. Neville is already slipping and catching himself. Draco picks his way as if the mud were a personal insult. Harry and Ron are near the front, still in the clothes they wore on the train.

**Faces.** Neville is frightened of the slope. Draco is disdainful. Harry is staring ahead.

**Caption.** The steep path, from above.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 3

**Picture.** Black water, a fleet of small boats waiting with nobody in them, the castle doubled as lights on the surface. The class reaches the shore. No one has stepped in.

**Faces.** The boats are the new room. Harry looks as if the castle became real.

**Caption.** The lake opens.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 4

**Picture.** Hagrid moves along the shore, lamp high, lips moving in a count, a hand the size of a plate hovering over a boat. He has not given the order. Fang is not the point here. The boats knock softly.

**Faces.** He looks careful with them, which is new. The children whisper.

**Caption.** Hagrid was already counting heads.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 5

**Picture.** Hermione tells a nervous girl the boats are traditional, from a book. Ron tells Harry he heard the ceiling is enchanted, then doubts his brothers. Neville stares at the water and does not swim in his imagination, happily.

**Faces.** Whispers, cold breath. Hagrid is still counting, out of earshot of the punchline.

**Caption.** They talk in little knots, not to Hagrid yet.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 6

**Picture.** One empty boat noses the shore at Harry's feet. He looks at Hagrid, waiting for the word. The word has not come. The next panel is Hagrid telling them to mind the path, or the boats, as the old page starts.

**Faces.** Harry is ready. Ron is grinning at the water. Neville is not ready.

**Caption.** A boat bumps the shingle at Harry's shoes.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

#### Page 2 — The lake

##### Panel 7

**Picture.** Night on a steep muddy path. A chain of lanterns winds down through the trees. Hagrid’s lamp leads. Harry and Ron are near the front. Neville slips. Draco picks his way as if the mud is an insult.

**Faces.** Hagrid looks ahead, cheerful. Harry looks up, excited. Neville looks at the ground, worried. Draco sneers at Hagrid’s back.

**Caption.** First years follow Hagrid down a steep path.

**Dialogue.**

- Hagrid: Mind the path. It gets steep, and the forest isn't friendly.

**Art.** art/c6-p1-p1.jpg

##### Panel 8

**Picture.** The path opens on the lake. A fleet of little wooden boats rocks on black water with nobody rowing. Each holds four. Lanterns double in the still water. Hagrid waves them in.

**Faces.** Harry looks at the boats, full of wonder. Hermione is already curious how they move. Ron grins a let’s-go. Neville looks at the water, pale.

**Caption.** Small boats wait on black water.

**Dialogue.**

- Hagrid: No more than four to a boat. In you get.

**Art.** art/c6-p1-p2.jpg

##### Panel 9

**Picture.** Inside one boat on black water. Neville’s knuckles are white on the gunwale. Hermione sits straight, a hand on his sleeve. Ron’s knee bumps Harry’s. A toad makes a lump in Neville’s pocket.

**Faces.** Neville looks at the water, terrified. Hermione looks at him, steady and kind. Harry looks ahead, calm for someone else. Ron copies that calm.

**Caption.** Four to a boat. Neville clutches the sides.

**Dialogue.**

- Neville: I've never been in a boat. I can't swim.
- Hermione: Then hold the side, Neville, and don't look down.

**Art.** art/c6-p1-p3.jpg

##### Panel 10

**Picture.** From high above, the fleet slides by itself across the open lake, four by four, leaving clean lines. No splashing. Hagrid’s boat holds only him, lantern between his boots. Harry’s boat is near the front.

**Caption.** The boats move with nobody rowing.

- Ron: Nobody's rowing.
- Harry: They're moving anyway.

**Art.** art/c6-p1-p4.jpg

**Picture.** Hogwarts rises on the cliff, windows lit, doubled so perfectly in the lake that the boats seem to float between two castles. Harry stands in his boat. Ron grabs his robe so he will not fall.

**Faces.** Harry looks at the castle, undone by it. Ron grins. Hermione memorizes the towers. Neville looks up, fear forgotten.

**Caption.** Hogwarts rises, doubled in the lake.

**Dialogue.**

- Harry: That's Hogwarts.

**Art.** art/c6-p1-p5.jpg

##### Panel 12

**Picture.** Quiet on the water. Only Harry’s chin and hands show at the top of the panel. The lake holds his face among the lit windows, scar and glasses and all, and Ron’s red hair beside him.

**Faces.** The reflection looks wonderstruck and a little scared of wanting this. Both of them are silent.

**Caption.** Nobody speaks. Harry's face in the water.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** art/c6-p1-p6.jpg

**Caption.** They step under the candles.

#### Page 4 — The Hat’s rules

##### Panel 19

**Picture.** They enter the Great Hall and it explodes upward. Four long tables, hundreds of students, candles hanging in the air, and a ceiling that is the night sky. House banners: red, yellow, blue, and green. McGonagall leads in emerald robes.

**Faces.** Harry looks at the ceiling, awed. McGonagall looks ahead, brisk. She has seen this sky for years.

**Caption.** The Great Hall. Candles and a night-sky ceiling.

- Harry: The ceiling. It's the sky.
- Ron: Enchanted. Fred told me. He might even have been right.

##### Panel 20

**Picture.** A pearly ghost in a ruff and tights rises through a bench. Plates do not move. Neville jumps. Hermione steadies her books. Harry and Ron flank them.

**Caption.** A ghost drifts through a table. Neville yelps.

- Neville: It went through the table!

##### Panel 21

**Picture.** Nearly Headless Nick, pearly and bluish-white, Elizabethan ruff, drifts above the Gryffindor table. His head sits a little wrong, which is only the hint. He has already passed through the wood. Neville is still pale from the yelp.

**Faces.** Nick looks delighted to have an audience. Neville looks unready for the rest of the joke.

**Caption.** The ghost drifts back, pleased to be asked.

**Dialogue.**

- Nearly Headless Nick: Nearly headless, my dear new students. Do look.

##### Panel 22

**Picture.** Nick's pale hands close on his own head, gentle, like a man adjusting a hat. The ruff shifts. The neck is the point and we are about to see it.

**Faces.** He is proud. Hermione leans in, forensic. Ron leans back.

**Caption.** He takes his head in both hands.

**Dialogue.** None.

##### Panel 23

**Picture.** He tips the head. The cut is almost through. A single strip of pearly neck holds the head on. It is gross and it is the joke. Not a wobbly hint. The head is truly hanging.

**Faces.** Nick looks mild, as if this were manners. Neville goes green.

**Caption.** The neck is one strip.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 24

**Picture.** The head hangs aside by that strip, ruff askew, still talking-capable, still polite. A drop of nothing, because ghosts do not drip, but the picture is still disgusting. Students who know it grin. First years do not.

**Faces.** Nick is cheerful. Neville looks like he might sit down on the floor. Harry stares. Hermione stares and does not scream. Draco looks as if being shocked is beneath him, and fails.

**Caption.** The head hangs.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

#### Page 5 — Nearly Headless Nick

##### Panel 25

**Picture.** Nick settles the head almost straight. It remains a little wrong, on purpose. He smooths the ruff. The strip is hidden again and everyone knows it is there.

**Faces.** He is satisfied. Neville breathes. Ron's mouth is still open.

**Caption.** He sets it back, still nearly off.

**Dialogue.**

- Nearly Headless Nick: A single strip. I keep hoping for a cleaner cut.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 26

**Picture.** Nick drifts higher, proud, head on badly. McGonagall is a stern emerald shape with a scroll, not yet calling names. The stool is still empty in this moment, or far. The joke is over. The night goes back to being large.

**Faces.** McGonagall is not amused for long. The first years pull themselves together.

**Caption.** The Hall remembers it has a Sorting to do.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 27

**Picture.** McGonagall stands before the first years with a scroll, indicating the banners. Behind her a stool and an old patched hat wait, not yet alive. The four house colors frame her.

**Faces.** She looks along the line, fair and severe. Hermione stares at the hat. Draco is already home in the green banner. Harry does not know which color is his.

**Caption.** None.

**Dialogue.**

- McGonagall: You will be Sorted into Gryffindor, Hufflepuff, Ravenclaw, or Slytherin. The Sorting Hat decides. Not you.

**Art.** art/c6-p2-p3.jpg

##### Panel 28

**Picture.** Close on the Sorting Hat. The brim ripples. A tear near the brim opens like a mouth. The stool creaks. McGonagall’s hand has just left it.

**Faces.** The rip shapes a sly, knowing almost-smile. A first year flinches.

**Caption.** The Sorting Hat is set on a stool and twitches.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** art/c6-p2-p4.jpg

##### Panel 29

**Picture.** Dumbledore stands, very tall and thin, long silver hair and beard, half-moon spectacles, deep purple robes with silver stars, pointed hat. Both hands open toward the Hall. He is speaking to the children as a group. No one is walking to the stool yet.

**Faces.** He looks along the first years, kind and public, not chatting to one boy.

**Caption.** He stands at the staff table, hands open.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 30

**Picture.** Wide: four long tables and four banners, all visible. Gryffindor red and gold, Hufflepuff yellow and black, Ravenclaw blue and bronze, Slytherin green and silver. Older students in those colors. Dumbledore's hands include all four. The staff table is at the far end. Snape sits in black. Quirrell's purple turban is a face-only note, no second face.

**Faces.** Dumbledore is welcoming a whole room. Harry looks at the four colors and does not know which is his.

**Caption.** Four tables, and he indicates them.

**Dialogue.**

- Dumbledore: Welcome. This year you will belong to Gryffindor, Hufflepuff, Ravenclaw, or Slytherin.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

#### Page 6 — The four houses

##### Panel 31

**Picture.** Ron, travel clothes, maroon jumper, looks at the red and gold table with hope he is trying to hide. Fred and George are already there, grinning back. Harry looks from red to green.

**Faces.** Ron is hopeful. Harry is unsure. A red-table twin mouths something rude and fond.

**Caption.** Ron looks at the red table.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 32

**Picture.** Hermione, books against her chest, watches Dumbledore, attentive, not performing. Draco looks past him at the green and silver table as if the speech were a delay.

**Faces.** Hermione is locked on the speaker. Draco has already chosen a color with his eyes.

**Caption.** Hermione looks at Dumbledore. Draco looks at green.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 33

**Picture.** McGonagall, formal, emerald, square spectacles, eyes on the roll of names. She is not the one welcoming. The Hat sits on the stool, quiet, leather face slack. No one has walked forward.

**Faces.** She is patient and exact. The Hat is not singing in this picture.

**Caption.** McGonagall looks at the scroll, waiting.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 34

**Picture.** Dumbledore's hands lower. The four tables murmur. The first years stay a knot. The painful-death warning is not this speech and does not happen here. The next old panel can be the Hat's song, and the first name is still ahead.

**Faces.** Dumbledore looks kind and finished. Harry looks at the Hat. No one has said Potter.

**Caption.** The welcome ends. The list has not started.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 35

**Picture.** The Hat sings from the stool. In the candle smoke, four shapes appear: a lion, a badger, an eagle, and a snake. McGonagall waits beside it. Dumbledore watches from the high table. The hall leans in.

**Faces.** The Hat looks proud of its song. Dumbledore twinkles, kind. Harry looks from the lion to the snake, unsettled. Draco looks at the snake, satisfied.

**Caption.** The Hat sings.

**Dialogue.**

- Sorting Hat: I look for courage, and for a loyal heart. For a keen mind, and for ambition. Put me on, and I shall Sort you.

##### Panel 36

**Picture.** A small student who is not one of them crosses a huge empty stretch of floor. The hat waits. Hundreds of faces are a blur of eyes. Harry, Ron, Hermione, Neville, and Draco watch the walk they will all have to make.

**Faces.** The walking child swallows, looking at the hat. McGonagall looks at her scroll, neutral. Harry measures the empty floor. Ron looks at his own shoes.

**Caption.** The first name. One child walks up alone.

**Dialogue.**

- McGonagall: Abbott, Hannah.

#### Page 7 — Names

##### Panel 37

**Picture.** The Hat is only just on Hermione’s bushy hair and already finished. She lifts it off herself, smiling, and the red table erupts. McGonagall nods once.

**Faces.** Hermione looks toward Gryffindor, relieved, a true smile, then back at Harry and Ron. Draco looks bored.

**Caption.** The Hat barely touches her. Gryffindor.

**Dialogue.**

- Sorting Hat: Gryffindor!

**Art.** art/c6-p3-p1.jpg

##### Panel 38

**Picture.** Draco sits on the stool as if it were a throne. The Hat touches his platinum hair and agrees at once. Green and silver banners brighten. Crabbe and Goyle wait. Snape, at the high table, gives the smallest approval.

**Faces.** Draco looks past the Hat at Harry, a cool smile. Snape looks satisfied. Harry looks back, unimpressed.

**Caption.** Draco asks for Slytherin and gets it at once.

**Dialogue.**

- Draco: Slytherin. Make it Slytherin.
- Sorting Hat: Slytherin.

**Art.** art/c6-p3-p2.jpg

##### Panel 39

**Picture.** Draco is seated among the Slytherins, a goblet in his hand, turned fully around. The unsorted line is far off. Harry is the one in focus between Ron and Neville.

**Faces.** Draco’s smile is thin, a challenge and an invitation. Harry looks forward at the hat and will not look back. Ron scowls.

**Caption.** From the green table, Draco looks back.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** art/c6-p3-p3.jpg

##### Panel 40

**Picture.** Neville is on his hands and knees halfway to the stool, robes tangled, Trevor in a pocket. A moment later the same boy is under the Hat, and the Gryffindor table is ready to cheer. Hermione is already standing to make room.

**Faces.** On the floor he is mortified, ears red. Under the Hat he beams, disbelieving. Hermione welcomes him. Harry is glad.

**Caption.** Neville trips. Then the Hat shouts Gryffindor.

**Dialogue.**

- Sorting Hat: Gryffindor!
- Neville: I did it. I actually did it.

**Art.** art/c6-p3-p4.jpg

##### Panel 41

**Picture.** Ron’s hem is twisted into a rope, his ears as red as his hair. The Hat comes off in his hands as he sprints for the red table. Hermione and Neville make room. Harry is still in the unsorted line.

**Faces.** On the walk Ron is scared of being last, or of Slytherin. When the Hat shouts, the worry breaks into a grin. Harry’s small smile says he is scared too, and glad.

**Caption.** The Hat shouts Gryffindor for Ron.

**Dialogue.**

- Ron: Not last. And not Slytherin. Please.
- Sorting Hat: Gryffindor!

**Art.** art/c6-p3-p5.jpg

##### Panel 42

**Picture.** McGonagall’s mouth is open on the name. The entire hall has turned. Even the candles seem to lean. Harry is a small figure at the start of that huge attention. The stool waits. Ron is half-risen on the red bench. Dumbledore and Snape watch from the high table.

**Faces.** Harry’s throat is tight. Ron wills him over. Snape is unreadable and intense. Dumbledore is gentle. McGonagall has a flicker of warmth.

**Caption.** The hall goes quiet.

**Dialogue.**

- McGonagall: Potter, Harry.

**Art.** art/c6-p3-p6.jpg

#### Page 8 — The choice

##### Panel 43

**Picture.** Inside the Hat it is almost black. Only a rim of candlelight shows at the bottom. The brim covers Harry’s eyes. His scar and his tense mouth sit below the leather. His hands grip the stool.

**Faces.** His eyes are hidden. He looks into the dark of the brim as if someone is standing there.

**Caption.** The Hat covers his eyes.

**Dialogue.**

- Sorting Hat: Hmm. Difficult. Plenty of courage. And a thirst to prove yourself. Interesting.

**Art.** art/c6-p4-p1.jpg

##### Panel 44

**Picture.** A split feeling. In the dark, a colder Harry wears finer robes with a green lining and does not look kind. On the stool, the real Harry shakes his head under the Hat. The red table, where Ron and Hermione sit, is the color he wants.

**Faces.** His jaw is set. The vision looks proud and alone. He refuses it.

**Caption.** Harry thinks no.

**Dialogue.**

- Sorting Hat: Slytherin would suit you, you know. Ambition. A certain disregard for rules.
- Harry: No. Not that house. Anywhere but Slytherin.

**Art.** art/c6-p4-p2.jpg

##### Panel 45

**Picture.** A pause, then the Hat’s brim flies wide. Harry flinches at his own result. A red banner hangs behind him. McGonagall is a step away.

**Faces.** His eyes show again as the brim lifts: shock, then relief. He looks toward Gryffindor. McGonagall allows a brief warmth.

**Caption.** A pause. Then the Hat shouts Gryffindor.

**Dialogue.**

- Sorting Hat: Very well. Gryffindor!

**Art.** art/c6-p4-p3.jpg

##### Panel 46

**Picture.** The red table explodes. Hands pull Harry onto the bench. Fred and George stand on it, yelling. Ron pounds the wood hard enough to jump the plates. Hermione claps. Neville beams further down. Pumpkin juice spills.

**Faces.** Ron’s worry is gone, pure joy. Harry grins, belonging. The twins are loud and welcoming. Hermione looks happy and right.

**Caption.** The red table explodes.

**Dialogue.**

- Fred: We've got Potter!
- George: About time, Ron, shove up!

**Art.** art/c6-p4-p4.jpg

##### Panel 47

**Picture.** The feast has appeared: roast, pies, piles of plates. Dumbledore stands at the high table, arms open, then one finger raised. In the foreground Ron’s laugh is caught and dropped, a goblet paused at his mouth. Snape watches Harry, not the food. Quirrell, in a purple turban, claps a beat late.

**Faces.** Dumbledore is kind, and his eyes are not joking. Ron’s laugh dies into a question. Harry snags on the warning. Hermione is already filing it away.

**Caption.** Ron's laugh stops.

**Dialogue.**

- Dumbledore: Welcome to a new year at Hogwarts. Before the feast, one warning. The third-floor corridor, on the right-hand side, is out of bounds to all who do not wish to die a most painful death.
- Ron: He's joking. Tell me he's joking.

**Art.** art/c6-p4-p5.jpg

##### Panel 48

**Picture.** Across the feast, through steam and candles, Snape’s face is locked on Harry. Harry has a fork halfway up, a roast potato on it, and has gone still. Everyone else is eating and blurred.

**Faces.** Snape does not blink. The dislike feels personal. Harry looks back, the belonging of the last moment punctured. He does not look away first.

**Caption.** Snape stares at Harry and does not blink.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** art/c6-p4-p6.jpg

## Chapter 7 — First Lessons

### Scene — The moving stair

One place, one stretch of time. Wardrobe holds for the scene unless a panel shows the change.

#### Page 1 — Morning in the stairwell

##### Panel 1

**Picture.** Hogwarts in the morning, high windows, stone, portraits dozing in their frames. Harry and Ron hurry with timetables, school robes now, red-and-gold ties, gray V-necks. They have been Sorted. They are looking for a classroom, not falling yet.

**Faces.** Harry is determined. Ron is already unsure of the map.

**Caption.** A corridor of morning windows, the day after the feast.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 2

**Picture.** A tall stairwell, three-quarter view from a landing, low enough that a whole flight is visible, bottom and top. The flight is still. Both railings are part of it. Students climb it normally. No gap. No swing. Other flights are still too.

**Faces.** The climbers look bored, late, ordinary. Nobody is losing balance.

**Caption.** The stairwell from a landing, and the flight is still.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 3

**Picture.** Harry checks a timetable. The landing they need is across one still flight. A suit of armor stands with a spear. A portrait in a ruff has not leaned out. Peeves is not here.

**Faces.** Ron looks hopeful. Harry looks at the far landing.

**Caption.** They step toward the flight they want.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 4

**Picture.** Two older students on the stairs compare ink stains and laugh. A girl runs with a telescope. The castle is awake and unhelpful. Harry and Ron wait for a gap in the traffic.

**Faces.** The older students are at home. The first years are not.

**Caption.** Other students were already chatting on the way to class.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 5

**Picture.** A painted knight sleeps in his frame, helmet down. The armor's visor is dark. Ron opens his mouth to ask the way and shuts it. They are about to step on.

**Faces.** Ron looks like he nearly asked a painting for directions. Harry is already moving.

**Caption.** A portrait snores. The armor does not help.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 6

**Picture.** Harry's shoe on the first step. The flight is one solid object, hinged somewhere below, but it has not moved. Ron follows. Students on the other landings are not staring yet. The next picture is the swing.

**Faces.** They look ordinary, about to be late, not yet alarmed.

**Caption.** They put a foot on the still flight.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

#### Page 2 — The flight turns

##### Panel 7

**Picture.** A tall Hogwarts stairwell in the morning. Three-quarter view from a landing, low enough to see the bottom hinge and the top end mid-swing. One entire flight turns as a single solid object, pivoting at the bottom like a door hinged at the floor. Every step stays fixed to the other steps. Both railings turn with the flight. The upper end has swung away from the landing Harry and Ron wanted, and a gap is opening. Harry and Ron are on that flight, almost losing balance: feet planted, bodies jerked sideways, hands grabbing the railing that turns with them, a book sliding, robes flying. Students on the landings have stopped and stare. A suit of armor watches with a spear. Other flights are still.

**Faces.** Harry looks at the departing landing, alarmed, almost falling. Ron looks at Harry, a wild grin that is also panic, off balance. Students on the landings stare at the turning flight. The armor looks unimpressed.

**Caption.** One flight pivots at the bottom. They miss their landing.

**Dialogue.**

- Ron: That was our landing!
- Harry: It moved. The whole staircase moved.

**Art.** art/c7-p1-p1.jpg

##### Panel 8

**Picture.** Same stairwell, same morning, the camera still low and three-quarter so the bottom hinge and the swinging top are both in frame. The flight is one solid object, both railings turning with it. A book slides off a step. Harry and Ron, black school robes, red-and-gold ties, pointed hats still on because the stairwell is cold, are jerked sideways, hands on the moving rail. Students on the landing they are leaving have stopped and stare. No other flight is moving.

**Faces.** Harry is almost falling, eyes on the gap. Ron’s grin is panic. The students on the landing stare, nobody calm on the flight.

**Caption.** The book slides. The landing stares.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 9

**Picture.** Close on Harry’s hand locked on the railing that is turning with him. Robe sleeve, taped glasses at the edge of frame, scar on the right brow. The hinge is still visible at the bottom of the wider shot’s logic: the rail is part of the flight, not a separate bar. Steps stay fixed to each other.

**Faces.** His knuckles are white. He is not walking. He is holding on.

**Caption.** He grabs the rail that turns with him.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 10

**Picture.** A Hogwarts landing, still morning, still high up. The flight they were on has stopped. It is one solid staircase, hinged at the bottom, now resting against the wrong arch. The gap it left is empty. Harry and Ron stand on the landing, not on the flight. Other flights are still. A suit of armor watches.

**Faces.** Harry looks at the wrong arch, working it out. Ron looks at the stopped flight, half a grin and half panic. The armor does not care.

**Caption.** The flight stops against the wrong arch.

**Dialogue.**

- Ron: It's stopped. On the wrong floor.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 11

**Picture.** They step off onto the wrong landing. The flight is still, hinged at the bottom, resting on the wrong arch, gap left behind it. Harry and Ron are on the stone, not on the steps. Students who stared are already walking a different way. Armor with a spear has not moved.

**Faces.** Harry looks back at the stopped flight, working it out. Ron is half a grin and half late. They are off it.

**Caption.** They get off on the wrong landing.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 12

**Picture.** The stairwell behind them, one flight still. Ahead, the mouth of a portrait corridor. They have not entered it. Morning light from a high window. Hats still on. Books clutched.

**Faces.** Harry looks at the next corridor, not relieved. Ron looks at the stair as if it might follow.

**Caption.** The stair stays behind them.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 31

**Picture.** The Transfiguration classroom door bangs. Harry and Ron fall in dusty, the wastebasket still on Ron’s head. ##### Panel 19

**Caption.** Hermione waves harder. Draco smirks.

**Dialogue.**

- Hermione: Sir, asphodel and wormwood make the Draught of Living Death. A bezoar comes from the stomach of a goat. It cures most poisons.

**Art.** art/c7-p3-p5.jpg

##### Panel 21

**Picture.** Extreme close in torchlight. Snape’s face fills one side, greasy hair, hooked nose, black eyes, no blink. Harry’s scar prickles. Sweat sits at his hairline. His fingers find the scar where Snape cannot quite see.

**Faces.** Snape looks at the scar itself, not only the boy. Harry looks into those eyes and does not understand the hatred. The pain is small and private.

**Caption.** Harry's scar prickles.

**Dialogue.**

- Snape: Monkshood and wolfsbane, Mr Potter. The difference, if you please.
- Harry: I don't know, sir.
- Snape: There is none. They are the same plant. Also called aconite. How disappointing.

**Art.** art/c7-p3-p6.jpg

##### Panel 22

**Picture.** The dungeon table. Harry half-stands and indicates Hermione, giving her the answer. Snape’s fingers flick as if removing something from the air. Hermione’s hand is down. Ron is silent beside them. Torches, bottles, a steaming cauldron.

**Faces.** Harry looks at Hermione, apologetic, then at Snape, confused. Snape looks satisfied, calling it arrogance. Hermione is grateful and dismayed. Ron hates him.

**Caption.** None.

**Dialogue.**

- Harry: Hermione knows the answer, sir. Ask her.
- Snape: Arrogance, Mr Potter. Five points from Gryffindor, for hiding behind a classmate.

**Art.** art/c7-p4-p1.jpg

##### Panel 23

**Picture.** Snape turns his back, robes flaring, chalk in one hand, a second flick of his fingers, done with them. The blackboard is blank. Draco and the bulky boys at the Slytherin table are pleased. Harry sits down hard, fists under the table. Ron and Hermione flank him.

**Faces.** Snape does not really look back. Draco looks victorious. Harry is angry now, not just embarrassed. Hermione blinks hard at the board.

**Caption.** None.

**Dialogue.**

- Snape: And five more, because you knew nothing at all. Sit down, Mr Potter.

**Art.** art/c7-p4-p2.jpg

##### Panel 24

**Picture.** A close profile of Snape at the board. The chalk is only a scribble, not words. His eye has slid sideways. Harry is a soft shape in the direction of that look, out of focus, among the jars and torch shadow.

**Faces.** Snape’s mouth is neutral. His eye is not. He looks at Harry with an old grievance Harry has not earned this week.

**Caption.** The dislike looks personal.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** art/c7-p4-p3.jpg

#### Page 5 — The melted cauldron

##### Panel 25

**Picture.** The Potions dungeon, same torches, no windows, same long tables. Class has moved on from the questions. Neville Longbottom, round face, sandy hair, ears going red, black robe and red-and-gold tie, stands at a cauldron that has started to smoke wrong. Harry has not touched it. Snape is at the board, back half turned. Draco watches from the Slytherin table, green-and-silver tie.

**Faces.** Neville looks terrified of his own potion. Harry looks at the smoke, not guilty. Hermione’s eyes are on the cauldron, already worried.

**Caption.** Neville’s cauldron starts to smoke.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 26

**Picture.** Close on Neville’s cauldron. The iron slumps. A thick potion runs over the rim and the metal folds like hot wax. Smoke boils up. His hands are off it. Harry’s hands are on his own untouched cauldron. No wand thread. This is the potion failing.

**Faces.** Neville’s mouth is open and nothing comes out. The cauldron looks ruined. Harry looks innocent and too near it.

**Caption.** The cauldron melts.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 27

**Picture.** The mess across the dungeon stones: melted iron, spilled potion, smoke at table height. Neville is horrified, ears scarlet, robe speckled. Seamus and the nearer Gryffindors lean away. Harry did not touch the cauldron. His own is cold and whole.

**Faces.** Neville looks like he might cry. Harry looks at his own clean hands. Draco’s smile is starting.

**Caption.** The mess. Harry did not touch it.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 28

**Picture.** Snape rounds on Harry, not on Neville. Black robes, greasy hair, sallow face, hooked nose, torchlight. He points at Harry across the smoke. Neville is still beside the ruined cauldron, ignored. Hermione’s hand is down because she has learned.

**Faces.** Snape looks at Harry with an old dislike, soft and mean. Harry looks back, confused. Neville looks at the floor. Draco is smug.

**Caption.** Snape blames the wrong boy.

**Dialogue.**

- Snape: Mr Potter. Five points from Gryffindor, for standing there while a cauldron dies.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 29

**Picture.** The Gryffindor table. Harry sits, points gone, jaw tight. Draco, at the green table, looks smug over a whole cauldron. Neville tries to mop with a sleeve and makes it worse. Snape has turned back to the board as if the matter were finished.

**Faces.** Harry is humiliated and angry. Neville is ashamed. Hermione is furious and silent. Draco enjoys the smoke.

**Caption.** Points off Harry. Draco is smug.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 30

**Picture.** Same dungeon, smoke thinning. They are still at the table. The ruined cauldron sits between Neville’s elbows. Torches. No windows. They have not left. Snape’s back is a black shape at the board.

**Faces.** The class looks exhausted. Harry looks at Neville, not at Snape. Neville will not look up.

**Caption.** The smoke thins. They are still in the dungeon.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

### Scene — The corridor after

One place, one stretch of time. Wardrobe holds for the scene unless a panel shows the change.

#### Page 6 — The corridor after

##### Panel 31

**Picture.** They step out of the dungeon door onto the stair they came down. The dark room is behind them. Afternoon light is somewhere above. Harry, Ron, and Hermione are at the bottom, just out, bags on shoulders. This is the walk out. Snape is not in the frame yet.

**Faces.** Ron is already talking, angry. Hermione is indignant and precise. Harry looks smaller than he did at breakfast.

**Caption.** They leave the room.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 32

**Picture.** After class they climb the dungeon stairs from torch-dark into a window’s afternoon light. Other students pass and glance. Ron talks with both hands. Hermione still has a book and is still arguing. Harry is quiet between them, bag on his shoulder.

**Faces.** Ron looks at Harry with loyal outrage. Hermione agrees and corrects him at the same time. Harry looks ahead, not at them, trying to find what he did.

**Caption.** After class.

**Dialogue.**

- Ron: He hates you. Proper hates you.
- Hermione: It was unfair. And it was obvious. That still isn't a reason.

**Art.** art/c7-p4-p4.jpg

##### Panel 33

**Picture.** The same stair from dark to a window, a little higher. Other students pass and glance. Ron is still using both hands. Hermione still has the book. Harry is still quiet between them. The dungeon door is below, out of the nice light.

**Faces.** Ron looks outraged on Harry's behalf. Hermione agrees with him and is about to correct the wording. A passing student looks curious and keeps walking.

**Caption.** Same stair. Other people notice.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 34

**Picture.** They stop at a corridor window. Lawns, the lake, and a distant willow are green below. Harry’s question hangs. Ron scratches his neck. Hermione closes her book against her chest, which means she does not know.

**Faces.** Harry looks from Ron to Hermione, genuinely asking, a little small. Ron looks at his shoes, frustrated. Hermione looks at Harry, sorry, intelligent and useless. None of them look at the view.

**Caption.** None.

**Dialogue.**

- Harry: What did I do to him?
- Ron: Be Harry Potter?
- Hermione: I haven't an answer. I hate that.

**Art.** art/c7-p4-p5.jpg

##### Panel 35

**Picture.** They walk on from the window along the bright corridor. Lawns still show through the glass behind them. The question has not been answered. Robes, bags, Ron's red hair, Hermione's book closed against her chest. They do not see the dark end of the corridor.

**Faces.** Harry looks ahead, troubled. Ron looks at him, frustrated that a joke was not enough. Hermione looks sorry and still without an answer.

**Caption.** They take the question with them.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 36

**Picture.** A long corridor, light at their end and dark at his, torches between. From behind, Snape is a black shape in the doorway, hands hidden, head turned toward Harry. The three friends are small in the window light, walking away in their robes, not seeing him. Ron’s red hair is the only warm color.

**Faces.** A slice of Snape’s face is cold, watching Harry’s back. He does not blink. The three look toward the day. Harry’s profile is troubled.

**Caption.** Down the corridor, Snape watches them go.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** art/c7-p4-p6.jpg

## Chapter 9 — Flying class and the Seeker

### Scene — The lawn at dawn

One place, one stretch of time. Wardrobe holds for the scene unless a panel shows the change.

#### Page 1 — Dawn on the lawn

##### Panel 1

**Picture.** Hogwarts from the lawn, dawn, dew, the castle pink. A class of first years trails out of a side door in cloaks and pointed hats, cold, brooms not in their hands yet.

**Faces.** They look sleepy and nervous. The day is beautiful and unhelpful.

**Caption.** The castle at dawn, pink stone, mist on the grass.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 2

**Picture.** Wooden school brooms lie flat in two lines. Madam Hooch walks the row, yellow hawk eyes, short gray hair, whistle, dark flying cloak, setting a broom straight with her boot. She has not said good morning.

**Faces.** She looks strict and wide awake. The brooms look like they might misbehave.

**Caption.** Brooms in two neat rows on the wet grass.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 3

**Picture.** Harry steps from the path onto the lawn, cloak hem dark with wet. Neville looks at the brooms as if they were dogs. Draco looks as if he has flown since birth. Hermione looks as if she has read the chapter and would prefer the ground.

**Faces.** Neville is afraid. Draco is smug. Harry is curious. Hooch has not spoken.

**Caption.** They come onto the grass.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 4

**Picture.** Wide: two rows of first years behind the brooms, castle behind, mist. All feet on the ground. A broom twitches and is still. Hooch stands at the end, whistle down.

**Faces.** The group is cold and whispering. She is about to make them stop.

**Caption.** The class, whole, two lines, nobody in the air.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 5

**Picture.** Draco tells Crabbe the school brooms are a joke, without a full line we need. Ron bristles. Neville whispers to Harry that his gran did not want him on a broom. Hermione shushes them because a teacher is present.

**Faces.** Whispers. Hooch is still out of hearing, fixing the last broom.

**Caption.** They were already arguing about who could fly.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 6

**Picture.** She faces them, yellow eyes, the class gone quiet by instinct. Brooms at their feet. Nobody has said up. The next panel is her good morning.

**Faces.** She looks ready. Neville looks ill. Harry looks straight ahead.

**Caption.** Hooch turns. The whistle is still down.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

#### Page 2 — Brooms

##### Panel 7

**Picture.** The Hogwarts lawn at dawn. Mist, dew, and a pink castle. Two rows of first years stand behind wooden brooms that lie flat on the grass. Madam Hooch, short silver hair and yellow hawk eyes, looks along the lines in a dark flying cloak. Harry studies his twiggy broom. Neville is already afraid of his. Draco, in green-lined robes, watches Neville.

**Faces.** Hooch is sharp. Harry is curious, a pull in his hands. Neville is afraid. Draco scents weakness. Hermione looks determined.

**Caption.** Dawn on the lawn. Brooms on the grass.

**Dialogue.**

- Madam Hooch: Brooms on the grass. Stand beside them. Good morning.

**Art.** art/c8-p1-p1.jpg

##### Panel 8

**Picture.** Close on the dew. Hooch holds one hand flat and a whistle near her lips. The students' hands hover over brooms that still lie on the grass. Neville's fingers twitch downward too early. Harry's hand stays correctly raised. Draco watches Neville, not the teacher.

**Faces.** Hooch is stern and careful. Neville wants the mistake over with. Draco's smile is starting. Harry listens.

**Caption.** Madam Hooch holds the class on the ground.

**Dialogue.**

- Madam Hooch: Right hand over the broom, and say up. Nobody kicks off until I give the word.

**Art.** art/c8-p1-p2.jpg

##### Panel 9

**Picture.** The class in cloaks, each right hand over a broom on the grass, the way she has already told them on the old page. They are waiting for one word. Neville's mouth is not the first mouth.

**Faces.** They are tense. Hooch's hand is up, not yet a stop.

**Caption.** Right hands over the brooms.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 10

**Picture.** Mouths open across the line. The word leaves them in a lump. Brooms jump off the grass all at once, badly, a hedge of sticks.

**Faces.** Excitement and immediate regret. Hooch's yellow eyes flash.

**Caption.** They shout it together.

**Dialogue.**

- Class: Up!

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 11

**Picture.** A single school broom launches vertically off the dew, too fast, handle first, toward a student's face. Not Neville. Neville is still on the ground in this picture, eyes wide, his own broom only twitching.

**Faces.** The victim has no time. Neville looks horrified that brooms do this.

**Caption.** One broom shoots straight up.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 12

**Picture.** The broom smacks the student's face. His head snaps. Hat askew. The laugh is the impact, not an injury that lingers past this morning. He sits down hard in the dew.

**Faces.** Shock, then a wail starting. The class flinches. Draco almost laughs and thinks better of it when Hooch looks.

**Caption.** It hits him full in the face.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

#### Page 3 — Up, and a face

##### Panel 13

**Picture.** The boy sits, cloak soaked, both hands on a red nose, eyes watering. The broom lies beside him like a guilty dog. No blood festival. A red mark is enough.

**Faces.** He looks offended by physics. Hooch looks furious and not surprised.

**Caption.** He sits in the dew, holding his nose.

**Dialogue.**

- Student: My nose!

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 14

**Picture.** Hooch points the class back, whistle at her lips, not yet the speech she gives Neville. Neville, round face, sandy hair, has his mouth open for his own up. His broom is eager. The face-hit does not replace his crash. The next panel is Neville too soon.

**Faces.** Neville looks like he cannot stop the word. Hooch has not reached him. Harry sees it coming.

**Caption.** Neville's mouth is already opening.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 15

**Picture.** Neville is yanked off the grass. His broom stands like a pole and hauls him, sandy hair blown, both hands locked on the handle, no glasses and no scar. Dew scatters. Hooch's whistle is a second late. Harry steps forward and stops. Draco laughs.

**Faces.** Neville looks down in terror. Hooch is alarmed and professional. Harry is worried. Draco is delighted.

**Caption.** Neville kicks off. The broom shoots up.

**Dialogue.**

- Neville: Up!
- Madam Hooch: Mr Longbottom, I did not say go!

##### Panel 16

**Picture.** From the ground, Neville is a struggling shape against the morning sky, circling because he cannot steer. One shoe has come loose. A tower stands at the edge of the sky. Below, Hooch shouts and Harry looks up, too small to help.

**Faces.** Neville's mouth is open, no pride left. He looks at the ground. Harry looks up, helpless. Hooch looks up, urgent.

**Caption.** He hangs on, circling, terrified.

**Dialogue.**

- Madam Hooch: Come down! Lean forward, Mr Longbottom, and come down this instant!

**Art.** art/c8-p1-p4.jpg

##### Panel 17

**Picture.** Neville has hit the grass on his back, robes tangled, one wrist clutched. The broom lies beside him. A small glass ball with white smoke inside rolls from his pocket. Hooch kneels. Harry and Hermione are close. Ron is pale. Draco looks at the ball, not at Neville.

**Faces.** Neville is crying and ashamed as well as hurt. Hooch is competent and concerned. Hermione is worried. Draco is interested only in the glass.

**Caption.** He falls. A crack. His wrist is wrong.

**Dialogue.**

- Neville: My wrist. I think it's broken.

**Art.** art/c8-p1-p5.jpg

##### Panel 18

**Picture.** Hooch supports Neville and points a strict finger at the class: stay down. Her yellow eyes promise consequences. The others stand frozen beside brooms on the grass, the castle doors behind her. The glass ball has stopped near Draco's shoe. Harry nods that he will stay.

**Faces.** Hooch is severe. Neville looks back, distressed. Draco looks at the ball, thoughtful. Harry means to obey.

**Caption.** Hooch takes him up to the castle.

**Dialogue.**

- Madam Hooch: Hospital wing for you. The rest of you, feet on the grass. One broom in the air and you are out of Hogwarts before you can sneeze.

### Scene — The theft and the catch

One place, one stretch of time. Wardrobe holds for the scene unless a panel shows the change.

#### Page 4 — The theft

##### Panel 19

**Picture.** Draco crouches on the dew and pinches the glass ball between two fingers, white smoke turning inside it. Bulky boys shadow him. Harry is a few steps away. Hermione stands with her arms folded. Ron glares at Draco's hand. Brooms lie abandoned. The castle has swallowed Hooch and Neville.

**Faces.** Draco looks at the ball, then at Harry, a game beginning. Harry knows it is not Draco's. Hermione warns. Ron is angry.

**Caption.** Draco picks up the fallen Remembrall.

**Dialogue.**

- Draco: What's this bit of glass Longbottom dropped?

**Art.** art/c8-p2-p1.jpg

##### Panel 20

**Picture.** Hermione steps in, chin up, both feet planted, smaller than Draco and clearer. He holds the glass ball just out of easy reach. Harry moves closer. Ron is at Harry's shoulder. The other students watch and do not take a side.

**Faces.** Hermione is stern and in the right. Draco is amused, not threatened, and checks whether Harry is watching. Harry is deciding.

**Caption.** None.

**Dialogue.**

- Hermione: It's a Remembrall. His grandmother sent it. Give it back, Malfoy.

**Art.** art/c8-p2-p2.jpg

##### Panel 21

**Picture.** Draco is astride a broom a foot off the grass, the Remembrall dangling, one finger crooked. Harry's own broom lies by his shoe. Hermione reaches for Harry's sleeve. Ron looks at the sky, torn. The class steps back from the forbidden air.

**Faces.** Draco looks down, baiting, smile sharp. The decision is already in Harry's shoulders. Hermione is alarmed and looks toward the castle.

**Caption.** Draco mounts, the ball in his hand.

**Dialogue.**

- Draco: Come and take it, Potter. If you can fly.

**Art.** art/c8-p2-p3.jpg

##### Panel 22

**Picture.** Harry swings a leg over the broom and it stirs under him before he kicks off. Hermione's hand catches empty air. Ron steps back to give him room. Draco waits above, delighted, the glass ball in his hand. Long dawn shadows cross the lawn.

**Faces.** Hermione pleads. Harry looks at the ball, not at her, sorry and already gone. Ron nods. Draco is delighted.

**Caption.** Harry mounts.

**Dialogue.**

- Hermione: Harry, no. Madam Hooch will have you expelled.

**Art.** art/c8-p2-p4.jpg

##### Panel 23

**Picture.** Harry rises. The lawn falls away. His body knows the broom. Hair and robes stream, and his face changes from stubborn to astonished joy. Tiny Draco is ahead with the glint of the ball. Tiny Hermione stands below with her hands on her head.

**Faces.** Harry looks forward with a wild gladness, like someone who has just found the sky. There is no fear in him.

**Caption.** He kicks off. The ground drops away.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** art/c8-p2-p5.jpg

**Faces.** Harry is calm, the joy banked into focus. Draco's smile is slipping. He is surprised to be matched.

**Caption.** He levels with Draco above the towers.

**Art.** art/c8-p2-p6.jpg

##### Panel 25

**Picture.** Above a stone courtyard, Draco's hand is open from the throw. The glass ball drops past Harry's knee toward the flagstones, small and getting smaller. Harry's head snaps down. Towers rise on either side. Tiny people are specks on the stone.

- Draco: Fetch, Potter.

- Hermione: Harry!

**Picture.** McGonagall stands on the castle steps in emerald robes and a pointed hat, one finger pointing at Harry. The courtyard goes quiet. Harry drifts in, dusty, the glass ball in his fist, the sky-joy gone. Ron and Hermione freeze. Draco lands apart, suddenly innocent, empty hands.

- McGonagall: I have found you a Seeker.
- Wood: A first year?

**Faces.** Wood's grin is a captain seeing a season. Hope arrives in Harry carefully. McGonagall is satisfied. The icy steps are rewritten.

**Caption.** Wood measures him, then grins.

- Wood: Small. Light on a broom. And look at that catching hand. You'll do, Potter.

**Faces.** Wood looks delighted. Harry has not understood the size of it. McGonagall looks satisfied and strict.

- Wood: Seeker. The smallest player, and the one the Snitch is for.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

#### Page 2 — The challenge

##### Panel 7

**Picture.** Afternoon in the castle entrance hall, doors open to the sun. A pale boy with slicked platinum hair and a green-lined robe steps into the path, hands behind his back, a polite false smile. Two stocky boys close in behind him. Harry, in a black robe with a satchel, stops. Ron measures the exit. Hermione, a step back, is already suspicious.

**Faces.** The platinum-haired boy looks pleasant and false. Harry is guarded. Ron is measuring the way out. Hermione is not buying the smile.

**Caption.** Draco stops him in the entrance hall.

**Dialogue.**

- Draco: Potter. A word. Without the Weasley, if you can manage it.

**Art.** art/c9-p1-p1.jpg

##### Panel 8

**Picture.** Under a suit of armor, the platinum-haired boy leans in and taps a watch, the challenge delivered like a gift. Harry's bag strap tightens in his fist. Ron leans in, already tempted. Sun and dust fill the quieter side of the hall.

**Faces.** The challenger's smile is small and bright with the trick. Harry weighs pride against sense. Ron is eager, and the decision is tilting the wrong way.

**Caption.** None.

**Dialogue.**

- Draco: A wizard's duel. Midnight. The trophy room. Wands only. No teachers, unless you're frightened.

**Art.** art/c9-p1-p2.jpg

##### Panel 9

**Picture.** Hermione plants herself with a book to her chest and counts the rules on her fingers. Harry looks past her. Ron looks defensive. Down the corridor, the platinum-haired boy walks away with the two stocky boys and does not look back.

**Faces.** Hermione is urgent, right, and annoyed that she has to be. Harry's pride prickles as he watches the receding back. Ron is defensive. The shoulders walking away look pleased.

**Caption.** None.

**Dialogue.**

- Hermione: It's a trap. And it breaks about fifty school rules, starting with curfew.

**Art.** art/c9-p1-p3.jpg

##### Panel 10

**Picture.** A window alcove in gold afternoon light, armor along the stone, the lawn outside. Ron's hands are open. Harry holds his satchel in both hands. Hermione is a frustrated shape walking ahead, out of the decision.

**Faces.** Ron is loyal and reckless, grinning. Harry is worried, and he nods anyway. The flying-courage is still in him.

**Caption.** Harry looks at Ron.

**Dialogue.**

- Ron: We have to go. If you don't, he'll tell the whole school you ran.
- Harry: All right. Midnight.

**Art.** art/c9-p1-p4.jpg

##### Panel 11

**Picture.** The same entrance hall, same afternoon sun on the flags. Ron nods. Harry’s satchel is in both hands. Hermione is ahead, book to her chest, not part of the nod. Draco is gone.

**Faces.** Ron is loyal and reckless. Harry is worried and agreed. Hermione is right and unheard.

**Caption.** Same hall. They will go.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Faces.** The hall looks ordinary. They look like students between classes. The night is not here.

**Caption.** Afternoon. Nobody is out of bed.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

### Scene — The corridor after

One place, one stretch of time. Wardrobe holds for the scene unless a panel shows the change.

#### Page 9 — Fluffy

##### Panel 49

**Picture.** Hermione's wand-light flares. One monstrous dog fills the stone room: three heads, three sets of teeth, one huge body, paws the size of doors, drool and leather collars. The four children are a strip of pajamas and one robe at the bottom. The toad leaps from Neville's hands.

**Faces.** All three heads look down, lips back. Harry looks up, awe and terror. Hermione looks at the paws, thinking even now. Ron stares at the middle teeth. Neville's face is going slack.

**Caption.** A dog the size of the room. Three heads.

**Dialogue.**

- Hermione: Lumos.
- Ron: That's not a dog. That's three dogs.

**Art.** art/c9-p3-p1.jpg

##### Panel 50

**Picture.** Each head snarls on its own: left furious, middle cruel, right barking. Neville's eyes roll and he goes down in a pale heap, the toad landing on his chest. Harry and Ron grab his arms. Hermione's wand-light jolts.

**Faces.** The heads are distracted between the fallen boy and the standing ones. Harry is urgent. Ron watches the head that looked at him. Hermione looks at the floor under the paws.

**Caption.** Neville faints.

**Dialogue.**

- Neville: I don't feel well.

**Art.** art/c9-p3-p2.jpg

##### Panel 51

**Picture.** Between the huge paws, a wooden trapdoor with an iron ring sits in claw-scratched stone. Harry crouches over fainted Neville and sees it. Hermione sees it a half-second later. A string of drool falls from the head above, near the ring. The dog is not loose in a room. It is a lid.

**Faces.** Harry understands, and the fear sharpens into a question. Hermione's mind is racing. The middle head looks down.

**Caption.** The paws are planted on a trapdoor.

**Dialogue.**

- Harry: Look under the paws. A trapdoor. It's standing guard on something.

**Art.** art/c9-p3-p3.jpg

##### Panel 52

**Picture.** A head snaps and comes away with cloth, not skin. Ron's maroon sleeve tears. He yanks Neville's ankle. Harry, glasses crooked, hauls Neville toward the door, one shoe already lost. Hermione is at the handle with the light in her other hand. The door is not open yet.

**Faces.** Ron is shocked at the teeth and at his sleeve. Harry looks at the door, pulling. The dog looks cheated. Hermione's face says come on.

**Caption.** They drag Neville. Ron's sleeve tears.

**Dialogue.**

- Ron: My sleeve! Pull, Harry, pull!

**Art.** art/c9-p3-p4.jpg

##### Panel 53

**Picture.** The door is shut. A huge impact bursts dust from the wood. On the safe side the four of them are a heap against it. Harry's hands stay on the latch. The toad hops clear. Moonlight comes through a high window. Neville is still out of it.

**Faces.** Harry looks at the booming wood, panting, alive. Hermione checks the latch. Ron checks Neville. Neville's face is slack, missing everything.

**Caption.** The door slams. A head hits the wood.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** art/c9-p3-p5.jpg

##### Panel 54

**Picture.** Quiet after the noise. They sit on the corridor floor against the still door. Harry holds a lost slipper. Ron's sleeve is torn, hair stuck to his forehead. Hermione's wand is dark. Neville is waking, the toad on his knee. Moonlight through a high window.

**Faces.** They look at the door, then at each other, shaky. Harry has a breath of a laugh that is mostly terror. Hermione is not ready to scold. Neville is confused, about to be told.

**Caption.** They listen until the growling fades.

**Dialogue.**

- Hermione: Has it stopped?
- Harry: I think so. Don't open it again.

**Art.** art/c9-p3-p6.jpg

#### Page 10 — The package

##### Panel 55

**Picture.** An alcove on the way back, a tapestry, the moon in a high window. Hermione crouches and draws in the dust: a square and a huge paw print over it. Harry, Ron with his torn sleeve, and Neville with the toad lean in.

**Faces.** Hermione is certain, voice low, and she looks up to make sure they see it. Harry nods at the paw. Ron believes it. Neville is lost, but he is listening.

**Caption.** None.

**Dialogue.**

- Hermione: That dog isn't guarding the room. It's guarding the trapdoor.

**Art.** art/c9-p4-p1.jpg

##### Panel 56

**Picture.** Harry sits back, one hand on his chest, the dusty paw print forgotten. Moonlight sits in his glasses. The others watch him. There is no package here. He is holding the memory of one.

**Faces.** Harry looks through them, remembering, uneasy. Ron waits. Hermione is ready to connect it. Neville looks at the toad, sleepy.

**Caption.** Harry remembers the package from Gringotts.

**Dialogue.**

- Harry: Hagrid took a parcel out of a high-security vault at Gringotts. He wouldn't tell me what it was.

**Art.** art/c9-p4-p2.jpg

##### Panel 57

**Picture.** A memory, softer and faded at the edges. Blue lantern light in a deep stone vault. Hagrid's big hands hold a grubby wrapped package at the mouth of his overcoat. Harry, small in a Muggle jacket, stands in the doorway with a coin pouch and watches.

**Faces.** Hagrid looks at the package, serious. Memory-Harry is curious. The feeling of the present is that this is the thing under the dog.

**Caption.** A flash of Hagrid hiding the parcel.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** art/c9-p4-p3.jpg

##### Panel 58

**Picture.** Back in the corridor. Ron stands, wrapping the torn sleeve, and points toward the warm arch that leads to the common room. Harry sits on the floor, still thinking. Hermione stands with her arms folded. Neville sways, half asleep, the toad in both hands.

**Faces.** Ron pleads to be ordinary, scared and covering it with practicality. Harry is not convinced. Hermione wants to agree and does not quite.

**Caption.** None.

**Dialogue.**

- Ron: Not our problem. We nearly got eaten. Bed.

**Art.** art/c9-p4-p4.jpg

##### Panel 59

**Picture.** Moonlight through a high window. Hermione offers Harry her hand, dust still on one finger, her face set. He takes it. Ron's shoulders drop. Neville is already drifting toward the dark with the toad, wanting his bed.

**Faces.** Hermione is grave, a shared responsibility, not thrilled. Harry agrees without a speech. Ron looks loyal and doomed. Neville wants his bed.

**Caption.** None.

**Dialogue.**

- Hermione: It became our problem the moment we saw it.

**Art.** art/c9-p4-p5.jpg

##### Panel 60

**Picture.** A long night corridor. Hermione, Neville with the toad, and Ron with the torn sleeve walk away toward the light. Harry is last, one slipper, and he has stopped. He looks back toward the dark stair and the floor above, the question staying with him.

**Faces.** The others look ahead, exhausted. Harry looks back, thoughtful, the package and the trapdoor in his eyes. His mouth is closed.

**Caption.** Harry looks up toward the third floor.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** art/c9-p4-p6.jpg

##### Panel 7

Crabbe and Goyle bellow. A circle of students laughs because it is easier than not. Harry stops at the edge with his bag, glasses and scar, tired of it. Ron stands beside him, ears going red.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 48

**Picture.** The huge head rears, wand still in the nose, a roar that blows Harry's hair. Mucus is a gleam, not the withdrawal yet. The club wobbles as Ron's spell takes it.

**Faces.** The troll is offended. Harry hangs on. Ron is shouting with his face, the spell already spoken.

**Caption.** The troll makes a sound the room hates.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

#### Page 9 — The wand and the nostril

##### Panel 49

**Picture.** Harry's feet slip on wet tile. Both hands on the wand handle. The nostril holds it. The club is in the air above the troll's own head, not dropped. The drop is the next old panel.

**Faces.** Harry is stuck in his own idea. Ron looks up at the club.

**Caption.** He cannot pull it yet.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 50

**Picture.** From the sinks: the wand in the nose, the club in the air. She is small, tears on her face, not saved yet.

**Faces.** She looks aghast and alive.

**Picture.** The club tips out of the hover toward the troll's skull. Harry is still attached by the wand. The impact is the old panel that follows. The mucus withdrawal is the page after that impact.

**Faces.** Harry braces. Ron's wand arm drops. The troll does not understand.

**Caption.** The club starts to fall. The wand stays in.

**Dialogue.** None.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 52

**Faces.** The troll's eyes squeeze shut, knees buckling. Ron looks on with wild hope. Harry looks up through his arms. Hermione looks at Ron, amazed.

**Caption.** The club drops on the troll's own head.

**Caption.** The wand comes out.

**Caption.** The wand, covered.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 56

**Picture.** Arm out, wand dangling slime. Ron takes a step back and almost laughs and does not, because the troll is not down yet. Hermione stares at the wand, then at Harry, then at the troll.

- Ron: That is the worst thing a wand has ever done.

**Caption.** The troll sways.

**Caption.** They are alive enough to be disgusted.

**Art.** Not drawn yet.

##### Panel 59

**Picture.** The troll is a fallen hill on the tile, face-down, the club rolled away. Water patters from a broken tap. Harry, Ron, and Hermione stand in a triangle around it, dripping, too surprised to celebrate. Hermione's book lies ruined in the puddle.

**Faces.** They look at the troll, panting. Harry is exhausted. Ron looks as if he might be sick or laugh. Hermione is safe and shaking, tears mixing with tap water.

**Caption.** The troll falls. Water, dust, and silence.

**Picture.** A quiet close group in the ruined bathroom. The troll lies huge and pathetic behind them. Water still runs. Hermione's fingers find the torn place on Ron's sleeve. Harry leans nearby, wand loose, a small tired smile. Nobody poses.

**Faces.** Hermione looks at Ron, grateful, the common-room hurt set aside. Ron looks at her hand, ashamed and relieved. Harry looks at both of them. Alive is enough.

- Hermione: You came back for me.

### Scene — The lie

One place, one stretch of time. Wardrobe holds for the scene unless a panel shows the change.

#### Page 11 — The lie

##### Panel 61

**Picture.** The bathroom door fills with three teachers. McGonagall is in front, emerald robes, bun coming loose, spectacles, no hat, one finger out. Snape stands behind her in black, greasy hair, eyes on the room. Quirrell peers past them, turban straight, handkerchief at his nose. The troll lies in the water. Harry, Ron, and Hermione stand dripping in a line.

**Faces.** McGonagall looks at the children, anger born of fear. Snape looks at Harry, searching. Quirrell looks faint. Harry looks at McGonagall, bracing.

**Caption.** McGonagall, Snape, and Quirrell burst in.

**Dialogue.**

- McGonagall: What on earth were you thinking? A fully grown mountain troll. You might have been killed.

**Art.** art/c10-p4-p1.jpg

##### Panel 62

**Picture.** McGonagall's finger is aimed at the boys. Hermione steps in, smaller, chin up, and answers before they can. Ron's mouth is open and then stops. Harry stands stunned beside him. Snape watches, unconvinced. Quirrell hovers at the back. One fallen troll lies on the wet tile behind them.

**Faces.** McGonagall's anger pauses. Hermione looks up at her, determined. Harry looks startled by the step she takes. Ron looks guilty that she is doing this.

**Caption.** Hermione steps in front of the boys.

**Dialogue.**

- McGonagall: Explain yourselves, Potter, Weasley.
- Hermione: Professor, wait. Please.

**Art.** art/c10-p4-p2.jpg

##### Panel 63

**Picture.** Close. Water drips from Hermione's hair. Her hands are spread, empty, selling the lie in a steady voice. She does not look at the boys. McGonagall listens, spectacles a little fogged, green hat and robes. Harry and Ron stand like statues behind her.

**Faces.** Hermione looks at McGonagall, earnest, afraid she will not be believed. McGonagall reads it and chooses to allow it. Harry looks ahead, honoring the lie by silence.

**Caption.** None.

**Dialogue.**

- Hermione: I went looking for the troll. I thought I could manage it. They came to stop me being killed. The fault is mine.

**Art.** art/c10-p4-p3.jpg

##### Panel 64

**Picture.** A wide torchlit corridor outside the bathroom, the door shut, wet footprints on the stone. McGonagall, composed again in emerald and her pointed hat, delivers the points with one open hand. Hermione stands punished and proud. Harry and Ron look uncomfortable at being rewarded. Snape's mouth is a cut. Quirrell claps weakly and looks at the floor.

**Faces.** McGonagall looks stern and fond at once. Hermione accepts the loss of points. Snape does not believe a word of it. Harry looks uneasy with the reward.

**Caption.** Points off Hermione. Points to the boys.

**Dialogue.**

- McGonagall: Five points from Gryffindor, Miss Granger, for foolishness. Five points each to Potter and Weasley, for sheer nerve.
- Snape: How very convenient a story.

**Art.** art/c10-p4-p4.jpg

##### Panel 65

**Picture.** Farther down the torchlit corridor. Snape walks away, black robes swinging, and a dark red stain shows on the cloth at his calf. Harry has stopped in profile, hands at his sides, eyes on that stain. Ahead, Ron and Hermione walk on and do not see. Quirrell's purple turban bobs farthest away.

**Faces.** Harry's suspicion clicks into place, mouth closed. Snape does not look back. The others are already leaving the moment behind.

**Caption.** Blood soaks through Snape's trouser leg.

**Dialogue.**

- Harry: His leg. That's blood, not water.

**Art.** art/c10-p4-p5.jpg

##### Panel 66

**Picture.** Later. The common room is almost empty, firelight and red hangings. Three chairs are pulled close. Hermione has a towel on her hair and a mug. Ron, maroon sweater, ears still red, holds a mug. Harry has a blanket on his shoulders and a mug in both hands. The group is a triangle that will hold.

**Faces.** Ron looks at Hermione, shy thanks. Hermione looks back, a small smile, the insult forgiven if not forgotten. Harry looks at both of them, content. They look like friends.

**Caption.** Later, the three of them sit together.

**Dialogue.**

- Ron: Thanks. For the lie.
- Hermione: Thanks for the troll. Friends, then.

**Art.** art/c10-p4-p6.jpg
